- **graphify**: `graphify-out/` holds a knowledge graph built from the code alone: every `.nix` file's imports, functions, and `dotfiles.*` options (which module `defines` each, which hosts `configure` it, and which modules `reference` it), plus the Markdown headings. - **graphify-comparison**: One real question per source. "Without" is the search output plus the files a careful agent reads to answer. For the Julia and pstack questions the same model also answered both evidence sets; its prompt plus completion totals are shown in br ([toddwyder/julia-next](https://github.com/toddwyder/julia-next/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/graphify-comparison.md))
